1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

美時 (1795 TT)  Trading Buy

2026 年毛利率回升，海外 ECB 再融資計畫落地將降低利
息負擔。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優
化趨勢確立。營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升；
(2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集收割期 ， 重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib
預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 EPS 12.08 元，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利
仍受到併購融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億，毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提
升，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。費用顯著
攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴張所致。  
營業外收支部分， 1H26 非營業費用 25.76 億，其中財務成本達
23.67 億 (YoY+980.82%) ，主要受到美國較高利率環境下產生龐
大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣匯兌損失衝擊。
所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，若排除該項
一次性影響，調整後有效稅率為 22.3% 。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心 ：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注 、 7 月起併入 Sandoz
菲律賓業務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與
Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年
營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率
回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥
出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在 52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將
積極推進債務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、
顯著降低下半年及未來的財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性
並提升資金調度靈活度。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
美時之企業使命「提供全球病患更好、更安全且更負擔得起的藥物」。為追
求使命，同時投入環境與社會之永續發展， 2024 年初，美時已成立「永續
暨風險管理小組」 ，針對公司的永續發展工作建立管理機制，研擬永續發展
相關之短、中、長期目標、策略，並管理監督跨部門之執行過程與成果，以
從系統性的內部管理，極小化美時產品對於社會及環境所帶來的潛在衝擊。
公司簡介：  
美時專精於高門檻的複雜學名藥及癌症用藥的研發、生產與銷售。公司產品
線涵蓋中樞神經系統、癌症、心血管疾病等領域，並在美國、歐洲及亞洲多
國市場擁有廣泛的銷售通路。  
透過策略性併購與全球市場的布局，美時已逐步從區域性藥廠轉型為具備
全球影響力的國際製藥公司。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利仍受到併購
融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億， 1H26 累計營收 174.53 億 (YoY+84.18%) ，創下歷史半
年度新高紀錄。 2Q26 毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提升 3 個百分
點，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。 1H26 營業費用合計
48.22 億 (YoY+93.65%) ，其中管銷費用為 42.96 億 (YoY+97.06%) 、研發費用
為 5.26 億 (YoY+69.68%) ，費用顯著攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴
張所致。  
營業外收支部分 ，1H26 非營業費用合計高達 25.76 億 ， 其中財務成本達23.67
億 (YoY+980.82%) ，其他營業外淨支出為 2.09 億 (YoY-51.40%) ，主要受到美
國較高利率環境下產生龐大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣
匯兌損失衝擊。所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，使本期
實際有效稅率暫時提升至 29.5% ；若排除該項一次性影響，調整後有效稅率
為 22.3% 。  
區域營收佔比與年增：

收大幅增長 180% 達 68.97 億，營收佔比攀升至 62% ，成為公司最核心的營

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

豐興(2015) 
區間操作, TP 68 (潛在報酬 6.58%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/31)：63.8 
Target Price：68

豐興 26Q2 雖營收年減 9.20% 至 75.87 億元，但受惠型鋼與條
線出貨成長及產品組合優化，毛利率攀升至 12.74%；加計業外認
列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻 ， 稅後淨利達 8.47 億元 ，YoY 26.61%，
EPS 為 1.45 元。  展望 2026 全年，受鋼筋出貨承壓影響，預
估營收為 272.14 億元 ，YoY -6.28%； 但在型鋼與條線出貨維穩，
以及第二季業外收益的挹注下 ， 預估全年稅後淨利將成長至 25.31 
億元，YoY 12.28%，預估全年 EPS 為 4.35 元。   
評價方面 ， 按照過往 75% 配息率估算 ，2027 年有望配發 3.2 元
現金股利， 隱含殖利率約 5.1%。 考量獲利雖優於 2025 年且具備
殖利率支撐 ， 但未來整體營運動能受限 ， 故給予歷史本淨比中緣位
階 1.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設定為 68 元，投資建議為區間操
作。 
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
豐興鋼鐵(2015)成立於 1969 年，為台灣主要電爐鋼廠之一，
也是國內唯一同時具備型鋼 、 條線及鋼筋三大產品線的煉軋合
一鋼鐵廠。 
產品組合方面，鋼筋約佔營收 54%，市占率約 14.4%、條線
佔 25%、型鋼佔 21%。產能配置方面，豐興擁有85 噸 DC 電
弧爐和 100 噸 AC 電弧爐各一座 ， 搭配三條直列式軋鋼設備，
年產能達 180 萬噸。公司採用垂直整合的生產模式，具備完
整的煉鋼到軋鋼生產線，不僅可自給自足鋼胚，更能有效控制
生產成本。在原料採購策略上，豐興採取分批小量採購的方
式，有效降低原料價格波動風險。主要原料廢鐵和鋼胚約占成
本的六到七成，

⚫ 鋼筋銷售下滑，營收年減 9.20%至 75.87 億，EPS 1.45 元 
26Q2 鋼筋銷售年減 2 成，型鋼出貨與條線出貨年增，合併營
收 75.87 億，QoQ 18.47% ，YoY -9.20%；獲利受出貨季增
且產品組合優化 ， 毛利率升至 12.74%， 季增 1.20 個百分點，
年增 1.33 個百分點；營業利益 7.34 億，QoQ 24.47%，YoY 
-0.90%；業外認列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻，稅後獲利攀升至  
8.47 億，QoQ 60.83%，YoY 26.61%；EPS 為 1.45 元。

型鋼需求表現堅挺，預期全年出貨呈現持平至小幅成長，條線
目前雖處庫存消化階段，且下游需求呈分化 （螺帽與機械零組
件相對穩健，螺絲與手工具偏保守） ，但兩項產品展現出強韌
的定價能力；在原料跌價時報價依然穩定，價格與利差具備實
質支撐，預期將成為後市營收與獲利的核心動能。

預估全年營收為 272.14 億元，YoY -6.28%。受惠於產品組
合優化，預估毛利率將提升至  12.71%，年增 1.18 個百分
點，營業利益達 25.91 億元，YoY 3.54% 。加計第二季轉投
資等業外收益的顯著挹注，預估稅後淨利將攀升至 25.31 億
元，YoY 12.28%， 全年 EPS 為 4.35 元 ， 每股淨值約 39.91 
元。

1 
2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/08           研 究 員 ：曾 智 楷(Kevin  Tseng ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 、獲利創高， EPS 7.43 元 
廣達 2Q26 營收 1.04 兆， QoQ +28.1% ，YoY +105.6% ，受 惠於
伺 服 器、 筆電 第二季 均 呈 現雙 位 數 的成長 。 產 品 組 合 部 分 ， 伺 服
器 佔 整 體 營 收 8 成 ， 其 中 ，AI 伺 服 器 對 整 體 伺 服 器 營 收 貢 獻約
75%~80% ，筆電 則佔 整體營收的 低雙位數百分比。 筆電部分，
2Q26 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬 台 ，QoQ + 15% ，YoY  -5%。2Q26 筆
電 出 貨 優 於預 期 ， 主 要 由 於客 戶 新 機 種 的 拉 貨 動 能 延 續 至 第 二
季， 以及 各 品 牌 客 戶預期 記 憶 體 價 格 持 續 上 漲而 提 前 拉 貨。 而獲
利 方 面，2Q26 營 業 毛 利520 .8 億，QoQ +34.6 %。YoY + 46.6 %，
毛 利 率 5.0%，QoQ +0.2ppts，YoY -2.0ppts，營 業 利 益342 .4 億，
QoQ +48.7 %，YoY +67.8 %，營 利 率 3.3 %，QoQ +0.45 ppts，YoY 
-0.75 ppts 。公 司 表 示，高階 AI 機櫃對 公司 毛 利 的 稀 釋，隨著 產品
組合優化 影響 逐步 降 低 ， 以 及 2Q26 費 用 率 降 至 歷 史 新 低 的
1.72% ， 帶 動 營 利 率 獲 得 改 善 。2Q26 業 外 淨 利 23.2 億 ， 稅 後 淨
利 286.5 億， QoQ +35.2 %，YoY + 69.9 %， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.43
元。

二、 3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公司 公告 7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 3Q26 ， 公司 預期 AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆 ，QoQ 
+1.5 %，YoY + 112.5 %， 毛 利 率 5.1%， 營 利 率 3.3%， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50 /31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但 由 於客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ，全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
投資評等：買進 
目標價格：440 元 
     14x 2027 EPS

參考價位：347.5 元 
      10.9x 2027 EPS 
     2026/09/07 收盤

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
2 
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率部分 ， 公司表示， 由於 下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調
今年 全 年 資 本 支 出 由 原 本 的300 億 元 至 400 億 元 ， 主 要 投 向 加 州
與 泰 國 AI 伺 服 器 產 能 。2026 年底 AI 產 能 相 較2025 年 底 達 成 翻
倍 成 長 的 目 標 維 持 不 變 。此 外 ， 先 前 公 告以 197 億 元 收 購 友 達 光
電 華 亞 廠，預 計 2027 年 第 四 季 完 成 交 割 點 交， 用 於2028 年台灣
AI 伺 服 器 產 能 倍 增 的 需 求 。預 估 公 司2026 全 年 營 收3.97 兆，YoY 
+87%，毛利率 5.0% ， 營 利 率 3.2%，稅後淨利 1062 億， YoY 
+41.7%，全 年 稅 後 EPS 2 7.50 元。預 估 公 司 2027 全 年 營 收5.04
兆，YoY + 27%，毛 利 率 4.8%，營 利 率 3.1%，稅 後 淨 利1221 億，
YoY + 15.0 %， 全 年 稅 後EPS 31.63 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 440 元 
受惠 於 AI 伺 服 器 的 強 勁 需 求 ， 公 司 維 持今 年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收三位
數成長 展 望，且隨 美 系 四 大CSP 資 本 支 出 展 望 維 持 正 向，GPU 、
ASIC 伺 服 器專案 持續 放量 ，將 有 望 挹 注 公 司 營 運 持 續 增 長，對 公
司 長 期 展 望 維 持 正 向 。以公司 202 7 年 EPS 來看 ， 目 前 股 價 交 易
於 10~11 倍 本 益 比 ， 評 價 仍 具 上 修 空 間 ， 給 予 買 進 投 資 建 議 ， 目
標 價 為 440 元(14x 202 7 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客
戶為 Tesla 、GM 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 鴻 海 、 緯 創 、 和 碩 、 仁 寶 電 腦
及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

圖 五 、2026Q2 全球 PC 出貨 量

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

二、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50/31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可

## Document 062: 260831_mq_MTK NV ECB

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_mq_MTK NV ECB.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-31
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flashnote
31 August 2026
MediaTek
NVDA and GOOG subscribe to MTK's US$3.9bn
ECB
What's new
• Today, MediaTek announced the largest ECB ever in the Taiwan market.
MediaTek announced today that it is issuing a US$3.9bn (>NT$120bn)
overseas convertible bond (ECB), the largest ECB ever from a Taiwan issuer
(see news here). NVIDIA subscribed US$3.5bn (~90%), which marks its
first major direct investment in a Taiwan-listed company. Alphabet also
subscribed (amount undisclosed). Per the announcement, the conversion
price is set at ~NT$4,500 (i.e. 115% of its 31 Aug close). Proceeds fund
foreign-currency material procurement, and the company disclosed a max
dilution of ~1.67%.
• NVIDIA and Alphabet subscribed. In a subsequent interview, NVIDIA and
MediaTek announced a deepened partnership across 1) AI infrastructure:
MediaTek adopts the NVLink Fusion platform, giving hyperscalers, CSPs
and frontier labs a prebuilt, pre-validated path to bring custom XPUs into
NVLink-connected rack-scale AI factories, with NVIDIA networking and CPUs
pulled through when MediaTek wins; 2) local AI computing: multi-generation
DGX Spark/RTX Spark PC roadmap; and 3) automotive: Dimensity Auto
with DRIVE AGX. MediaTek mgmt reiterated AI chip revenue of ~US$2bn
this year and its target of ~15% of an ~US$80bn TAM next year, which it
expects NVLink Fusion to support by shortening customers’ time to market.
Why it matters
• Endorsement of MediaTek's AI strategy. NVIDIA mgmt commented that
the investment formalises a partnership built on a decade-long roadmap,
and it is NVIDIA's first major investment in a Taiwan-listed company. We
believe a commitment of this scale is hard to repeat regularly, positioning
MediaTek as NVIDIA's partner of choice in the custom-XPU expansion,
marking an endorsement that peers are unlikely to obtain and confirmation
of MediaTek's expansion from AI ASIC chips into full rack-scale systems and
platforms.
• Dilution is small, and overhang risk is low. Disclosed max dilution is only
~1.67%. Moreover, >90% of the deal sits with strategic subscribers (i.e.
NVIDIA, Alphabet), who we believe will not run the delta-hedging (short-
selling) that typically pressure shares after an ECB issuance.
• Partnership enlarges MediaTek's ASIC opportunity. Through NVLink
Fusion, every MediaTek XPU rack pulls through NVIDIA switching, networking
& CPUs, so NVIDIA gains from MediaTek's ASIC success. We believe NVIDIA
chose MediaTek because it is open to both GPU-attached & ASIC business,
whereas peers compete with NVIDIA in networking or are less aligned. This
supports mgmt's target of ~15% of an US$80bn TAM next year.
What now
• Reiterate Outperform, TP at NT$10,000.
Semicons & Semicon Equip
Taiwan

Lai

Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Memory Innovation –  The Focus
from SEMICON Taiwan
SEMICON Taiwan highlighted memory value creation shifting
from bit scaling toward architecture-level innovation. OW on AP
Memory for emerging 3D WoW stacking. OW on Montage for
memory interface opportunities. OW on Macronix for SLC
NAND.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
For a broader discussion of these technologies, please refer to our Global Insight:
Innovating the Next-Generation Memory.
Memory is becoming a system-level differentiator: AI performance is increasingly
constrained by memory rather than computing. Longer context, agentic workflows,
and KV cache growth are raising the importance of bandwidth, capacity, and latency,
with tokens/$ and tokens/W becoming more relevant metrics. We see this

reinforcing the strategic importance of memory architecture as AI inference scales.
3D stacking is likely to be the future trend beyond today's 2.5D architecture:.
Samsung's zHBM and broader DRAM-on-logic concepts point to memory moving
from beside computinge toward vertically integrated architecture. This should
increase the importance of hybrid bonding, WoW integration, and thermal co-design.
We see AP Memory as a potential beneficiary of emerging 3D WoW stacking

opportunities.
Memory interfaces are becoming more strategic: As AI systems adopt
heterogeneous memory tiers across HBM, DRAM, CXL, SRAM and flash,
connectivity and control between computing and memory become complex. Google
mentioned that CXL is used to leverage old DDR4. We believe this raises the value
of memory interface solutions and reinforces our OW view on Montage.
(Continued in the following section)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Architecture-level innovations span memory interfaces 3D integration,
computing-near-memory, and new AI use cases on NAND.
Inference shifts Al bottleneck to memory bandwidth, capacity, and data
movement.
3D stacking is likely to be the next major architecture beyond today's 2.5D HBM,
supporting new opportunities for Top Pick AP Memory.

NAND moves up the Al memory hierarchy; creates new opportunities beyond
storage.
Memory and computing are converging through PIM, custom HBM, and near-
memory architectures.
September 1, 2026 06:03 PM GMT

### Page 2

2
Overview (cont'd)
NAND is gaining a more active role in Al systems: Fast NAND, high-IOPS SSDs, and HBF
are moving closer to computing for KV cache, model weights, and inference workloads,
broadening NAND's role beyond storage. We see this as supportive for higher-performance
NAND and potential incremental SLC NAND demand, reinforcing our OW view on
Macronix.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)

Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost
of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) medium-term
growth rate of 16.0%, 3) payout ratio of 67%, 4) terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other

design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Montage Technology Co Ltd (6809.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n 8.4% CoE (1.2 beta, 3.0% risk-free rate, and 4.5% risk premium)
n 30% payout ratio
n 19.3% medium-term growth rate
n 4% terminal growth rate

These assumptions reflect cloud capex growth, DRAM interface technology migration, and
Montage's strong position in China's datacenter semi localization. We then apply an exchange

n Faster-than-expected phase-out of US peers

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected DRAM interface technology migration
n Delay in new product launches

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

M
Update
AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+54%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Bizlink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales came in at NT$8,892mn (-8% MoM/+54% YoY)
• QTD reached 65% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$28,521mn (+23% QoQ/+55%
YoY) and consensus at NT$27,268mn (+17% QoQ/+48% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 revenue is tracking roughly in line with our estimate.
• Looking into 2H26, we expect HPC to remain the main growth driver, as

power interconnects should continue to see momentum from AI/general
servers. Meanwhile, data interconnects should grow along with new ASIC
model ramp and GPU sever demand.
• Moreover, semi cap equipment revenue growth should also sustain.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Ethan Jia
Research Associate
Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
AI semi capex spending in Taiwan remains strong into 2027 to
solve the supply chain bottleneck. Keep OW on TSMC,
MediaTek, Hon Precision, Allring, MPI, Winway, etc.
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - key message from the  CEO Forum
fireside chat  –  Taiwan is still one of the bottlenecks of AI infrastructure buildout.

On the stage, TSMC Deputy Co-COO Mr. Hou  said that he had never seen demand
growing and changing this frequently as it has over the past six months, while the
tool delivery from WFE partners are still not enough.  MediaTek CEO Mr. Tsai was
asking for more capacity support from ASE, TSMC, and Unimicron.
Why investors came to this trade show in Taiwan: We believe the tech industry
and capital markets are finding ways to keep AI hardware spending sustainable in

coming years. With memory becoming the major bottleneck and cost item for AI
capex, investors are checking whether technologies can improve AI computing
efficiency, for example, CPO to replace copper connectors in XPU scale-up. All in all,
the goal is to reduce per-token cost.  Please see following section for details.
Key focus areas during  SEMICON Taiwan:
1. Silicon photonics for AI networking and  testing process development : In
our previous report, AI Supply Chain: GPU Architecture Optimization Given

Memory Shortage and Supernode Challenge, we indicated that future GPU/
ASIC scale-up would depend on CPO/NPO (“inter-rack CPO connectivity
remains Nvidia's preferred direction”). For example, the NV576 Rubin Ultra in
2027 may use CPO switch trays to connect eight NVL72 server racks. Silicon
Photonics and PIC (Photonic integrated circuit), lasers, CPO, and related
testing present large investment opportunities.
2. Memory optimization from high cost : Instead of adding density, increasing

bandwidth and fully utilizing existing memory resources has become the key
technology trend. Our recent supply chain checks suggest that Rubin Ultra
CPU is highly likely to use 8-Hi HBM4, around one-third fewer than 12-Hi in
Rubin. But by increasing HBM4 base-die units and migrating to HBM4e, plus
some software optimization, total memory bandwidth should be sufficient
for a token output increase.
3. "More than Moore"- Panel-level packaging and Intel EMIB-T - We visited the

Toray (Toray Industries covered by Takato Watabe) booth to check EMIB-T
substrate manufacturing yield, because the company supplies the TCB
bonder.  We expect Intel to provide technical support, and expect MediaTek’s
TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate supply in 2028.
4. Although it wasn’t the main focus of the SEMICON , we think TSMC's
Moore’s Law migration (for energy efficiency) and MediaTek's ASIC enabling
(to save on chip budgets) are  keys to make AI capex more sustainable.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

Need for more capacity to support strong AI semi demand
into 2027
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - CEO Forum
The discussion focused on how strong AI demand is leading to ongoing undersupply from
the whole semi industry . We believe AI demand remains strong and is able to support
strong revenue growth for ASE, TSMC, and MediaTek in coming years. At the same time,
Taiwan's semi supply chain is also expanding capex and opex aggressively to catch up to

the demand.
Stock ideas around SEMICON Taiwan:We remain OW on TSMC given its dominance in
leading-edge foundry. MediaTek remains our Top Pick given its competitive ASIC design
service. We hosted a booth tour to visit Hon Precision, AllRing, MPI, and WinWay to cross-
check CoWoS and CoPoS expansion plans, as well as optical insertion technology. We are
OW on all the above stocks.
Key focus areas during SEMICON Taiwan 2026
1. Silicon Photonics for AI networking and the testing process development:

Please also see Semiconductor Production Equipment: SEMICON Taiwan Day 1 –
Takeaways from SiPh Seminar from Suzune Tamura. We share the view with Tamura-san
that testing is the key challenge for CPO  mass production. During SEMICON Taiwan,
several companies showcased their solutions or components  for CPO testing. Although
the solutions might be different, all companies were providing similar information: that
CPO testing is still not clearly defined while most of the companies are still trying to get

qualified by major customers with their equipment/solutions. The speed and precision of
optical alignment remains the key to throughput, and  we believe there is still room for
improvement.

know-how in aligning silicon-bridge bonding to PCB. We expect Intel to provide technical
support, and expect MediaTek’s TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate
supply in 2028. For 3D IC packaging, please also see Semiconductor Production

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

### Page 4

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 4
Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Stock prices in our  upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews
negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings based on 60% share/125k switches/9mn
FAUs, US$175 FAU ASP , US$1.58bn TAM).

Source: UBS Research
Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

Asia Vital Components
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the expansion of its addressable market.
Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the
company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment, rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing

value of thermal content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural drivers still in place, we expect
the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported by other
qualitative factors such as cold-plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2)

heightened price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling
technologies.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +1% MoM/+344%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on King Slide's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$6,466mn (+1% MoM/+344% YoY) – another record
high.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of MSe, at NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348%
YoY), and 77% of consensus, at NT$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimate.

• On top of the mainstream GPU/ASIC server demand from hyperscalers, we
believe that King Slide is generating more revenue from various projects in
different applications as well.
• We believe the company should continue to benefit from server market
growth and increasing dollar content, driven by more complex designs and
broader adoption of rail kits. We view it as a good proxy for the AI capex
cycle, given its dominant position, strong patent portfolio, and solid

execution.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 107: 260909_daiwa_testing-interface

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_testing-interface.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Summary: Driven by strong AI/HPC demand, all three companies set new revenue 
milestones: Winway (TWD 1.71bn, +6.6% MoM, +233.4% YoY), MPI (TWD 2.05bn, 
+13.4% MoM, +69.9% YoY), and CHPT (TWD 638m, +3.9% MoM, +54.3% YoY). With 
Winway’s capacity expansions tracking ahead of schedule, MPI set to begin shipping CPO 
insertion 3 products in 4Q26 alongside ASP hikes in late-3Q26, and CHPT nearing a 
qualification for its turnkey CPU ASIC project, the sector offers resilient sequential growth 
visibility through 2H26 and potential earnings upside heading into 2027.

What’s new: Following the conclusion of SEMICON Taiwan, we have observed a marked 
uptick in investor enquiries regarding the semiconductor test interface supply chain. As 
AI/HPC architectural transitions, and the increasing complexity of 2.5D/3D packaging drive 
structural content gains, investors have focused increasingly on capacity visibility and 
technological roadmaps across the socket and probe card segments. In the note, we recap 
the latest August monthly revenue figures and synthesize key market dynamics across our 
coverage universe.

WinWay Technology (6515 TT, TWD7,280, Buy [1]) 
• Aug revenue result: Monthly revenue reached TWD 1.71bn ( +6.6% MoM, +233.4% 
YoY), bringing 1-8M26 cumulative revenue to TWD 9.81bn (+97.9% YoY). Jul-Aug sales 
reached 72% of our 3Q26E revenue, tracking ahead of our expectations.

• Product mix & catalysts: Growth remains anchored by aggressive tape-outs from 
North American GPU leaders and cloud hyperscalers. Shipments of proprietary coaxial 
test sockets and advanced wafer-level vertical probe cards expanded sharply. While the 
market may expect meaningful revenue contributions from CPO sockets or probe cards, 
we believe it will account for mid-single digit in 2027E and high-single digit in 2028E.

• Outlook: The company maintained a constructive stance on order visibility and 
persistent demand-supply tightness. Management reaffirmed that 2Q26 marked the 
near-term cyclical trough, guiding for sequential revenue expansion on a QoQ basis 
moving forward. Our supply chain checks indicate that capacity expansion execution is 
tracking ahead of schedule compared with last month. With Winway poised to bring 
additional capacity online in 2027, we suggest investors monitor the company’s 2H26 
earnings call (mid-Nov) for further clarity on its 2027 capex and capacity roadmap.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD10,000, based on a 
target PER of 70x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS forecast. Based on our 
2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 36.9x/19.5x, vs. its previous 
highs of 80-90x. Downside risks: heavy reliance on major US AI chipmakers and; 2) 
intensifying competition that could erode design wins.

MPI Corporation (6223 TT, TWD5,615, Buy[1]) 
• Aug revenue result: Monthly revenue surpassed the TWD 2bn milestone for the first 
time, reaching TWD 2.05bn (+13.4% MoM, +69.9% YoY), bringing 1-8M26 cumulative 
revenue above TWD 13.0bn. Jul-Aug sales reached 63% of our 3Q26E revenue 
estimate, tracking slightly below our expectations.

• Product mix & catalysts: Vertical Probe Cards (VPC) and MEMS probe cards are 
operating at near-full capacity to support volume ramps in HPC, AI accelerators, and 
high-speed networking ICs. MPI’s AST (Advanced Semiconductor Test) and Thermal 
test chambers also maintained solid delivery momentum to European and US 
automotive and R&D laboratories. CPO insertion 3 engineering test equipment is 
scheduled for delivery in 4Q26, setting the stage for mass production orders in 1H27.

• Outlook: Although management’s muted 3Q26 guidance initially fuelled investor 
scepticism, and the cumulative 2M26 run-rate is tracking slightly behind our quarterly 
forecast, we view the market’s pessimism as overly conservative. Strong late-quarter 
shipment seasonality, coupled with concentrated September revenue recognition for 
high-end VPC/MEMS probe cards under the newly effective pricing schedule should 
help bridge the gap and keep 3Q26 on track to meet or exceed our top-line 
expectations. Looking into 4Q26 and early 2027, the commercial rollout of CPO 
insertion 3 equipment and continued certification progress for insertion 2 provide a multi-
year growth runaway and durable operating leverage.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD8,415, based on a 
target PER of 65x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS forecast. Based on our 
2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 42.9x/26.0x, vs. its peak of 80x. 
Downside risks: 1) Competitive and pricing pressure; and 2) delays due to AI/HPC 
slowdown.

Chunghwa Precision Test Tech (CHPT, 6510 TT, TWD3,610, Buy [1]) 
• Aug revenue result: August revenue totalled TWD 638m (+3.9% MoM, +54.3% YoY), 
bringing 1-8M26 cumulative revenue to TWD 4.25bn (+33.14% YoY). Jul-Aug sales 
reached 66% of our 3Q26E revenue, tracking in line with our expectations. In response 
to Taiwan Stock Exchange (TWSE) inquiries following recent share price volatility, CHPT 
disclosed its August financial results. Single-month EPS came in at TWD 5.29 (net 
income of TWD 174m, +95% YoY), implying a quarterly EPS run-rate of TWD 15.87 (vs 
Daiwa estimate of TWD 16.08 and market consensus of TWD 17.52)

• Product mix & catalysts: Top-line momentum was broad-based across both the AI 
GPU and ASIC segments. Management highlighted ongoing footprint expansion and 
continued efforts to identify additional sites to scale manufacturing capacity. Moving 
forward, steady deliveries of AI GPU test boards, alongside the forthcoming volume 
ramp of custom AI ASICs, should underpin sequential revenue growth.

• Outlook: Our supply chain checks indicate CHPT is competing for three AI/HPC probe 
card turnkey programs, comprising a CPU ASIC, an AI ASIC program for a US 
automotive client, and another AI ASIC program for Taiwanese clients. The automaker 
qualification process is progressing smoothly, and CHPT plans to ship engineering 
probe cards for validation in November, with validation results expected by end-2026. 
Given the superior content value of probe card solutions versus board-only products, a 
successful qualification would provide incremental top-line upside into late-2026, while 
serving as a significant catalyst for 2027 revenue upgrades and valuation expansion.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD4,000, based on a 
target PER of 45x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS forecast. Based on our 
2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 30.2x/24.0x, vs. its peak of 68x. 
Downside risks: 1) project delays and utilisation risk; and 2) loss of market share.

## Document 115: 3037欣興_1150831_BofA

Source PDF: all report pdf/3037欣興_1150831_BofA.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Unimicron

Additional thoughts on the investigation 
incident of “products’ origin”

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 1,400 TWD | Price: 1,110 TWD

Investigation more likely related to non-substrate side 
Following our first take on the investigation incident (August 28), we lay out our further 
thoughts in this report. While the company does not disclose specifically which products 
or production sites are under investigation, supply chain checks indicate it is more likely 
to be related to the PCB (non-substrate), as the investigation may have taken place in 
the Hejiang plant (which focuses on PCB/HDI). 
Concerns on high-end ABF substrate look overstated 
Given that Unimicron’s high-end ABF substrate capacity is all located in Taiwan, we 
believe the issue of “mislabeling the products’ origin” may not be associated with such 
business. Rather, it is more likely associated with the PCB/HDI business, since the firm 
operates the factories in both China and Taiwan for production (and there could be some 
workload sharing in between, which involves the export/import).  
Fundamental impact minimal but share price likely to react 
PCB/HDI accounts for only 15% of the firm’s 2026 capex, and the business contributes 
no more than 10% of Unimicron’s profits, based on our estimates. We thus expect no 
significant impact on the company’s fundamental outlook, yet the share price may still 
react negatively on Monday, as the relevant issue (label of product origin) could be quite 
sensitive under the requirement/focus by US authorities and customers.  
Positive stance remains backed by S/D in ABF substrate 
On high-end ABF substrate, we do not think it makes sense for the firm to source from 
peers’ overseas capacity, given (1) Unimicron’s relative strength in product quality; and 
(2) the ongoing industry-wide supply shortage. Maintain Buy given its position as a 
major supplier (20+% share) in high-end substrate market.

* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 5.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Unimicron (3037 TT) was founded in 1990 and listed on 
the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 1998. The firm manufactures 
a wide range of PCB and IC substrates for consumer 
electronics, industrial, and automotive. It is also the leading 
ABF supplier globally. The company merged with Phoenix 
Precision Technology (PPT, delisted) in 2009 and expanded 
its exposure in the IC substrate business. UMC is the 
biggest shareholder in Unimicron with a 13.3% stake.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Unimicron in view of strong 
demand from AI accelerators (especially ASICs), which we 
expect to boost the utilization rate of the ABF substrate 
business from 2H26, in turn bolstering the company's 
margin profile and profitability going into 2027.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 13.0x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
Unimicron (UMCRF) 
Our PO of NT$1,400 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E, which is roughly 1 standard deviation 
above the historical average of 20x since 2017. In our view, the multiple is appropriate, 
as we expect Unimicron's earnings to be supported by its high-end technology offerings, 
where end market demand seems more resilient.

Upside risks are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) greater PC and regular server demand, and (3) 
lower competition pressure.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) lower ASP content increase 
at AI server, and (3) bigger drop in the demand of consumer electronic products.

大銀微系統 (4576 TT)  Buy

先進封裝、矽光子相關商機 2H26 持續擴大，評等為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：受惠於半導體前段製程、 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單
挹注，以及矽光子檢測設備相關訂單數量自 07/2026 開始放大，
微米及奈米級定位系統 2H26 營收成長性可期，加上精密運動
及控制元件需求亦持續回溫，故群益的投資建議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，但受到匯兌損失影響，稅後淨利
QoQ-0.73% ：2Q26 營收 10.23 億元， QoQ+19.70% ，除精密運動
及控制元件營收動能續強外，微米及奈米級定位系統需求亦再
增溫，相關營收 5.34 億元， QoQ+17.20% ，雖然毛利率受到匯率
影響而降低至 37.10% ，但營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，惟業外表現
不及 1Q26(1Q26 認列 0.10 億元匯兌利益， 2Q26 認列 0.37 億元
匯兌損失 )，稅後淨利 1.15 億元， QoQ-0.73% 。  
2H26 營收展望佳： 展望 2H26 ，微米及奈米級定位系統營收成
長性仍可期，目前訂單能見度達 5~6 個月，動能主要來自半導
體相關產品，原因有二， (1) 由於前段製程設備大廠訂單穩定挹
注，此部份商機主要來自台灣晶圓代工廠持續擴充先進製程，
(2) 台灣設備供應商 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單數量持續增加，
且 CoPoS 、FOPLP 測試設備相關產品已送樣至客戶認證。此外，
矽光子檢測設備相關訂單自 04/2026 開始出貨給台灣半導體設
備供應商，且數量自 07/2026 開始放大， 07~08/2026 達數百台，
客戶並於 08/2026 兩度追加訂單，訂單能見度達 3~4 季。  
為因應微米及奈米級定位系統需求增溫，大銀微陸續啟動兩階
段擴產作業，第 1 階段已於 06/2026 完成，產能擴大約 20% ，
第 2 階段則預計於 2026 年年底至 2027 年年初之間完成，屆時
產能將再擴大 20% ，均為營收持續成長的後盾。  
至於精密運動及控制元件 2H26 營收展望亦佳，包括中國、東南
亞 (新加坡、印度等 )等地 PCB 鑽孔機、成型機設備供應商相關
需求自 4Q25 開始回溫，目前訂單能見度為 2.5~3 個月，營收成
長性較佳的產品包括線性馬達、伺服馬達、驅動器等。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略而言，大銀微成立 「氣候變遷風險小
組」，由董事長主持，主導全公司減碳因應方針及短、中、長期減碳策略，
依循氣候相關財務揭露建議書 (TCFD) 架構以進行氣候風險及機會評估，並
透過暴露程度與脆弱度矩陣研擬調適行動計畫，分別進行氣候變遷風險分
析評估潛在財務衝擊。  
再就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，大銀微委託外部輔導團
隊協助教導 ISO 14064 溫室氣體盤查類別 3~類別 5 的執行項目，藉由盤查
間接溫室氣體所產生的碳排放數據，重整與規劃制定相關管理措施，使其降
低溫室氣體排放。至於大銀微持續進行節能減碳改善規劃方案則主要有五，
(1) 安裝節能智慧電表，結合節能 e 化系統管控電力使用，找出節能方案，
(2) 空壓機系統更換變頻空壓機或租賃馬力數較低的空氣用量， (3) 汰換冰水
機，選擇高效能的變頻冰水節能設備， (4) 分析找尋替代低碳及可回收材料，
並應用於產品循環經濟設計上， (5) 培養員工節能減碳的理念並養成節約用
電的好習慣。

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 800G DCI 於 2026 年進入批量出貨，2027 年需求放大；CW Laser 與 EML同步擴產，雷射
晶片月產能預計由 2026 年底約 6KK 顆提升至 2027 年底約 12KK顆，InP晶圓亦逐步由 2吋
轉向 3吋及 4吋。 研究部預估 2027 年營收 92.6 億元，毛利率 28.7%，EPS 13.5 元。目標
價上修到 807 元(60x2027(F)PER)，潛在漲幅 32.3%，投資評等上調至「買進」 。

位。

營 運 分 析 
華星光由 InP晶片延伸至光模組與高速光學封裝 
華星光（4979）成立於 2001 年，主要從事光通訊主動元件、InP（Indium 
Phosphide，磷化銦）晶片、光學次模組及高速光收發模組製造，製程涵蓋
Wafer Fab、Die Fab、Packaging，以及模組 Assembly與 Testing，具備由
晶片製造延伸至模組組裝測試的垂直整合能力。InP產品以 CW Laser、EML
及 APD為主，模組端則涵蓋 100G、400G 及 800G DCI 產品，並持續開發
1.6T。 
目前模組及封裝為主要營收來源，約占 8 成，InP 晶片約占 2 成。DCI 模組
維持單一主要客戶模式，雙方合作已14年且目前仍為獨家供應；InP晶片則
自 2026 年起增加客戶與產品種類，除既有CW Laser 外，EML及 APD亦陸
續進入量產。公司目前員工約 700 人，截至 26Q2 資本額約 14億元。  
近年營運重心由既有 DCI 模組代工及 CW Laser，逐步往 800G D CI、高速
EML、多波長與高功率 CW Laser，以及 NPO 光學封裝延伸。晶圓尺寸同步
由 2吋往 3吋、4吋移轉，並規劃4 至 6吋自動化產線；封裝端則已投入1.6T 
NPO製程及量產設備開發，預計27H2 進入量產，後續技術路線再往更高速
NPO及 CPO 延伸。

個股報告 
26Q2 認列時點差異壓低毛利率，26H2新品放量推升營收及獲利  
26Q2 營收 12.89 億元 （QoQ +8.0%、YoY +18.5%） ，毛利率22.4%，較26Q1
下降 3.0個百分點，營業利益 2.36 億元，稅後淨利1.84 億元，EPS 1.29元。
毛利率季減主要受到一筆財稅認列時點差異跨季影響，相關項目延至 26Q3
認列，目前未揭露該項目的金額及具體會計科目。26H1 累計營收 24.83 億
元、YoY +15.2%，稅後淨利 4.14 億元、YoY +34.4%，EPS 2.91元。 
26Q2 稅負亦高於正常水準。26H1 所得稅費用 1.21 億元，正常稅率以 20%
為基礎，惟 6 月另有未分配盈餘相關所得稅，使 26Q2 單季實質所得稅率升
至約 25.6%；研究部於 26H2及 2027 年回歸 20%正常化稅率估算。 
7月營收 5.09 億元、YoY +31.0%，創單月新高，稅前淨利 1.56 億元、稅後
淨利 1.31 億元，EPS 0.92元；前 7月累計營收 29.91 億元、YoY +17.6%，
前 7月 EPS約 3.83 元。7月獲利已開始反映 26H2 新品出貨增加。

26H2 營收增量主要來自 800G 及其他 DCI 新產品、既有 DCI、CW Laser
與 EML，其中 EML 已於 26H2 開始量產。研究部預估 26Q3 營收 16.31 億
元（QoQ +26.5%、YoY +51.5%） ，除前述跨季項目於Q3 認列外，隨新品
出貨及產能利用率提高，毛利率回升至 29.0%，EPS 2.33 元；26Q4 營收進
一步增至 19.05 億元 （QoQ +16.8%、YoY +65.0%） ， 毛利率28.6%，EPS 2.77
元。研究部預估 26H2 營收 35.36 億元，較 26H1 增加約 42%，與下半年新
品集中放量的出貨節奏一致。 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 60.18 億元、YoY +37.2%，毛利率26.8%、較 2025
年增加 5.8 個百分點，營業利益 13.78 億元、營業利益率 22.9%，稅後淨利
11.39億元、YoY +49.1%，EPS 8.0 元。全年毛利率提升主要反映 26H2高
速 DCI、CW Laser 及 EML出貨增加，以及營收規模放大後產能利用率改善；
26Q3 另有跨季認列項目回補，因此單季毛利率略高於 26Q4。

AI 資料中心規格升級，800G DCI於 2027年進入主要放量期 
資料中心高速互連需求持續上修，法說引用 LightCounting 資料顯示，2031
年 Intra-Data Center 市場規模預估由約 520 億美元提高至 800 億美元 。 整體
800G 光收發模組市場 2026 年需求已由先前預估約 3,500 萬支提高至約
7,000 萬支，1.6T 則由 2026 年約 1,500 萬支增至 2027年約 6,000 萬支，產
品規格持續往更高速、單模、多波長及高功率演進。 
DCI方面，2026 年仍以 400G 為主流，800G 已開始批量生產，2027 年進入
主要放量階段。法說引用市場資料估 2027 年 400G 與 800G DCI 合計需求
接近 200 萬支，其中800G 接近 100 萬支，2028 年增加至約 130 萬支、2029
年約 150 萬支；400G 於 2026 至 2027 年大致持平，2028 年後逐步下降。

華星光 DCI 100G、400G 與 800G 均已進入量產，800G 於 2026 年開始批
量出貨，2027 年客戶需求高於先前規劃，後續限制由訂單轉為供貨能力。依
目前產品出貨規劃，2027 年新增 DCI 產品營收可接近目前既有 DCI 業務規
模，既有產品同步成長下，整體 DCI 營收具一倍以上成長空間；2028 年新

- 4 - 
個股報告 
品營收規模則進一步擴大至目前既有產品的數倍，目前訂單能見度延伸至
2028 年底。

CW Laser 與 EML同步擴產，InP產能由 2吋往 3吋、4吋升級 
InP 產品線由 CW Laser、EML及 APD構成。CW Laser 目前已具 16 個波
長供應能力，相較一般單波或 4 波產品可覆蓋更多波長組合，多波長產品亦
具較佳毛利結構；EML 於 26H2開始量產，產品規格由100G 往 200G 升級，
主要對應高速、高功率及長距離單模應用；高增益APD亦於 26H2 進入量產
導入，應用包含車載光通訊。市場資料預估 2026 年 CW Laser 整體晶片需
求約 4億顆，EML相關需求約 1.6 億通道。

產能方面，CW Laser 與 EML 後段製程同步擴充，不因 EML 放量排擠 CW 
Laser。 雷射晶片月產能預計由2026年底約 6KK顆提高至 2027年底約 12KK
顆，2027 年底較 2026 年底增加一倍。 
晶圓尺寸同步由 2吋往 3吋、4吋移轉，2027 年以 3吋為主要生產平台，再
逐步增加 4 吋比重；4 至 6 吋自動化設備已進入規劃，預計 27Q4 起陸續到
位。產品規格方面，CW Laser 由 70／100mW 往更高功率延伸，並布局
400mW 搭配 SOA產品，EML則由 100G 往 200G 升級。晶圓尺寸放大、製
程自動化與產品規格提升共同支撐 2027 年 InP產量增加。

NPO於 27H2量產，高精度封裝能力往 CPO延伸 
華星光在既有 InP雷射、光元件封裝與DCI模組基礎上，進一步布局NPO。
1.6T NPO 目前進行封裝製程及量產設備開發，並與客戶及台、日設備廠共
同開發，預計27Q2 前完成開發、27H2 進入量產，2028 年開始較大量貢獻；
3.2T NPO 亦已展開前期開發，中長期技術路線再延伸至 CPO。 
NPO端已完成 Fiber Array 及 Laser Array 貼合 prototype，量產設備仍與客
戶及設備廠共同開發；Active Alignment 精度可進入 0.1μm 以下，封裝能力
已由一般光元件組裝往高密度光學引擎所需的高精度貼合延伸。 
27H2 NPO量產對 2027 年財測的直接營收貢獻仍有限，較大量貢獻預計落
在 2028 年；2027 年營收與獲利成長仍主要由 800G 及新增 DCI產品、CW 
Laser 與 EML擴產帶動，NPO則為 2028 年後新增成長來源。

廠區進度逐步明確，2027年 CAPEX超過 30億元 
產能擴張由不同廠區分別承接。合江廠逐步集中 InP Fab，規劃將既有空間
轉為晶圓製造用途，長期目標月產能約 3,000 片，後續再導入 4至 6吋自動
化產線；八德廠主要承接 DCI 模組 Assembly 與 Testing，並規劃保稅工廠
功能；吉林廠及新增空間則以 Packaging 與 NPO 相關產品為主，使 InP、
DCI與新封裝產品逐步形成獨立的產能配置。 
無塵室面積自 2024年底約 4,000平方公尺持續擴充 ，2025年新增合江廠 950

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

800G 與 InP產能擴張支撐 2027年成長，佈局 NPO/CPO未來展望正向 
研究部預估華星光 2026 年營收 60.2 億元、YoY +37.2%，毛利率 26.8%、
年增 5.8個百分點，營業利益13.8 億元，營業利益率22.9%，稅後淨利 11.4
億元、YoY +49.1%，EPS 8.0 元。26H2 隨 800G 及其他 DCI新產品、既有
DCI、CW Laser 與 EML出貨增加，預估營收及毛利率均較 26H1 提升，其
中 26Q3 另有前季跨季認列項目於本季反映。 
2027 年 800G DCI 進入主要放量期，新增 DCI 產品營收規模預計接近目前
既有 DCI產品， 加上既有產品持續成長 ；InP 端 CW Laser 與 EML同步擴產，
雷射晶片月產能由 2026 年底約 6KK 顆逐步提升至 2027 年底約 12KK 顆，
並提高 3吋晶圓生產比重 。 研究部預估2027年營收 92.6億元 、YoY +53.9%，
毛利率 28.7%、年增 1.9 個百分點，營業利益 23.4 億元、YoY +69.7%，營
業利益率 25.2%， 稅後淨利19.2 億元 、YoY +68.1%，EPS 13.5元。1.6T NPO
預計 27H2 量產，初期營收貢獻有限，較明顯貢獻預計自 2028 年開始。
